Function active_scores(c,h,n,tol):
  cmax=max(c); active_idx = np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
  If active count 0 or all? tol raw maybe cmax*1e-4. For scaled gradient, values? We'll use group.

      left 0..n-L-1: g=-h[L:]
      right L..n-1: g=1-h[:n-L]
    For m=0: g=1-2h.
    Then upper = np.maximum(upper, g); lower = np.minimum(lower, g).
  After active, if some active none for coordinate? Every coordinate may not appear in some lag; g for affected lags only? For m where coordinate not involved, derivative 0. Even better, we can maintain V incrementally under pair updates: updating h_p, h_q changes v for coordinates that are neighbors of p/q? v_i depends on h at positions i±m for all m; changing h_p affects v_i for i such that i±m=p => all i? For v_i, lags where p is neighbor: first/second. For each i, p can be i+m or i-m, so m = p-i (valid if within n). There is exactly at most 2 lags where h_p affects v_i. So updating h_p from old to new changes v_i by δ times ∂v_i/∂h_p: derivative of v_i wrt h_{i+m} is -1 for first? Let's compute v_i[m] = 1 - h_{i+m} if i first; = -h_{i-m} if i second; m=0 1-2h_i. So if h_p changes by δ, v_i changes:
- for m=p-i (i first, m valid): -δ
- for m=i-p (i second? h_{i-m}=h_p => -δ) (derivative wrt h_p of -h_{i-m} is -δ)
- for m=0 if i=p: -2δ (both)
Thus updating V all i for each p/q is O(n) each (2 rows), plus row p/q? Also row p changes due own h at m=0: v_p[0]=1-2h_p changes -2δ; and v_p other lags depend on h at p±m? Those h not changed except q? v_p lags involving q? If q neighbor, v_p changes due h_q in first/second. But if we just recompute rows p and q? General update:
When h_p +=δ, h_q -=δ:
For V (n,N), for each coordinate i:
 - If i first at lag m=p-i: V[i, p-i] -= δ (if m>0? for m sign? v_i uses signed m; if p>i, m=p-i, first condition; if p<i, m=p-i negative, second condition? In either case contribution -h_{neighbor}; derivative -1. So if m=p-i valid, V[i, idx(m)] -=δ.)
 - If i=p: V[p,0] += -2δ (self); if q similarly.
 - If i=q: V[q,0] -=? q change -δ, self derivative -2(-δ)=+2δ.
But also if i=p and neighbor q at m=q-p: V[p] has contributions including h_q? The same LCP? Let's use general derivative: v_i depends on h_j for j = i±m (neighbor) with coefficient -1 (except i=m? self coefficient -2). So for any changed coordinate r with change δ_r, for each i and m where neighbor = r, V[i,m] += -δ_r. The self case r=i,m=0 derivative -2δ, but if we also apply neighbor? For m=0 neighbor same as self; coefficient -2. So update: For each r,δ:
  m = r - i (if r as neighbor first/second for coordinate i): V[i,m] += -δ
  if i==r: V[i,0] += -δ (extra) to make -2δ.
This is O(n) per r: for all i, m=r-i valid; index = n-1 + (r-i). For i from r-n+1 to r, V[i, n-1+r-i] -=δ. This sets a diagonal in V. For r=p δ, r=q -δ.
Thus we can maintain V incrementally exactly! Initial V compute O(n^2) maybe 1M operations. Updates O(n) per pair if we update V (n=700) plus pair_exact O(n*ternary). V shape: rows i (coordinate), columns k corresponding signed m = k-(n-1), value ∂c_k/∂h_i (raw). For each i:
 m=0: 1-2h_i.
 For each neighbor j? c_m derivative wrt h_i:
  if m = j - i (i first, j=i+m): +1 - h_j
  if m = i - j (i second, j=i-m): -h_j
Thus row i has for each offset d=j-i (nonzero) contributions at m=d (first:1-h_j) and m=-d (second:-h_j). So to compute V efficiently:
Initialize V zeros n x N.

For each i, j loop? O(n^2): for d=1..n-1:
  positions i=0..n-1-d: m=d: V[i, n-1+d] += 1 - h[i+d]
  positions i=d..n-1: m=-d: V[i, n-1-d] += -h[i+d? wait j=i-d, h_j]
Better vectorized per d:
  V[:n-d, n-1+d] += 1 - h[d:n]
  V[d:n, n-1-d] -= h[0:n-d]

Pseudo `try_pair(V, c, h, p, q, active, active_margin, cmax, a_buf, val_buf, n)`:
  if p==q: return False, cmax
  tlo = max(-h[p], h[q]-1.0)
  thi = min(1-h[p], h[q])
  if tlo >= thi: return False,cmax
  np.subtract(V[p], V[q], out=a_buf)
  # first-order skip
  if active is not None and active.size>0:
     av = a_buf[active]
     right = np.max(av)
     left = np.max(-av)
  else:
     right = left = 0
  if thi > 0 and tlo < 0:
     if right >= -1e-13 and left >= -1e-13: return False,cmax
  elif thi > 0:
     if right >= -1e-13: return False,cmax
  elif tlo < 0:
     if left >= -1e-13: return False,cmax
  else: return False
  # ternary
  center=n-1; d1=center+q-p; d2=center+p-q;
  lo=tlo; hi=thi; bestf=cmax; bestt=0.0
  for _ in range(ITER):
    if hi-lo < 1e-14: break
    m1=lo+(hi-lo)/3; m2=hi-(hi-lo)/3
    # eval m1
    np.multiply(a_buf,m1,out=val_buf); val_buf += c; val_buf[d1]+=m1*m1; val_buf[d2]+=m1*m1; f1=val_buf.max()
    # eval m2
    np.multiply(a_buf,m2,out=val_buf); val_buf += c; val_buf[d1]+=m2*m2; val_buf[d2]+=m2*m2; f2=val_buf.max()
    if f1 < f2: hi=m2
    else: lo=m1
  t=(lo+hi)/2
  # better maybe evaluate endpoints and t
  eval t, 0, endpoints? f convex, t fine. But due ternary, also check t=0? cmax.
  np.multiply(a_buf,t,out=val_buf); val_buf += c; val_buf[d1]+=t*t; val_buf[d2]+=t*t; f=val_buf.max()
  if f >= cmax - 1e-12: return False,cmax
  # apply
  c += a_buf*t
  c[d1] += t*t; c[d2] += t*t
  h[p] += t; h[q] -= t
  update_V(V,p,t,n); update_V(V,q,-t,n)
  newmax = c.max()
  return True,newmax

def optimize_fixed(h0,n,budget_s,rng):
  h = h0.copy(); c=compute_c_raw(h); V=build_V(h,n); cmax=c.max()
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax*dx
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
  ITER=35
  a_buf=np.empty(N); val_buf=np.empty(N)
  best_pair_attempts=0
  while time<deadline:
    if time - last_restart > no_improve_time:
       # perturb best_h with noise scale increasing?
       noise = ...
       h = project_feasible(best_h + noise, n)
       c=... V=...; cmax=c.max()
       last_restart=time
    # compute gradient every 100 attempts or if due
    ... candidates
    improved=False
    for (p,q) in candidates:
       if time>deadline: break
       imp,newmax=try_pair(V,c,h,p,q,cmax,a_buf,val_buf,n)
       if imp:
          cmax=newmax; improved=True
          if cmax < best_cmax: best_h=h.copy(); best_cmax=cmax
          no_imp=0; break? After update, candidate list stale; break to recompute? We can break inner and maybe recompute gradient if many.
    if not improved:
       no_imp +=1
       if no_imp > 1000: perturb
  return best_h, best_cmax*dx, c,V?
```

Compute gradient weights:
```
def gradient_from_V(V,c,cmax,alpha=50.0):
   s = alpha*(c-cmax)
   # find max s=0
   w = np.exp(s) # max 1
   # threshold maybe 1e-8
   w[w < 1e-8]=0
   tot=w.sum()
   if tot==0: w[ c==cmax ]=1...
   w/=tot
   return V @ w
```

Vectorized per L:
```
def feasible(L):
  lo=tlo; hi=thi
  # affines
  mask = aff_mask (precomputed bool length N, false for d1,d2,center)
  am=a[mask]; cm=c[mask]
  # constants
  zerom = np.abs(am) <= 1e-14
  if np.any(zerom):
     if np.max(cm[zerom]) > L: return False
  pos = am > 1e-14
  if np.any(pos): hi = min(hi, np.min((L - cm[pos]) / am[pos]))
  neg = am < -1e-14
  if np.any(neg): lo = max(lo, np.max((L - cm[neg]) / am[neg]))
  if lo > hi + 1e-14: return False
  # convex d1,d2: process index
  process_convex(d1,c[d1],a[d1],lo,hi) -> list intervals
  process_convex(d2,...)
  # concave center
  process_outside(center,c[center],a[center],...)
```

def feasible_inside(L):
  if zero_max > L: return False
  lo=tlo; hi=thi
  if pos_cnt:
     tmp = (L - ca_pos) / aa_pos
     b = tmp.min()
     if b < hi: hi=b
  if neg_cnt:
     tmp = (L - ca_neg) / aa_neg
     b = tmp.max()
     if b > lo: lo=b
  if lo > hi + 1e-14: return False
  # convex d1
  disc = a1a1 - 4*(L-c1)
  if disc >= 0:
     sqrtv=np.sqrt(max(0,disc)); r1=(-a1-sqrtv)/2; r2=(-a1+sqrtv)/2
     if r1 > lo: lo=r1
     if r2 < hi: hi=r2
     if lo > hi +1e-14: return False
  # d2 similar
  # concave center d=-2
  disc0 = a0*a0 - 8*(L-c0)
  if disc0 >=0:
     sqrtv=np.sqrt(max(0,disc0)); r1=(a0 - sqrtv)/4; r2=(a0 + sqrtv)/4
     if lo > r1 - 1e-14 and hi < r2 + 1e-14:
         # interval entirely inside forbidden? Need if lo > r1 and hi < r2 (strict). Use if lo - r1 > 1e-13 and r2 - hi > 1e-13 maybe.
         if lo - r1 > 1e-12 and r2 - hi > 1e-12: return False
     # Actually if interval crosses outside, feasible. If interval entirely inside (r1,r2), infeasible. If touches boundaries, feasible. If hi < r1 or lo > r2, feasible. If lo<r1 and hi>r2, feasible both sides. Only infeasif lo >= r1 and hi <= r2 (within eps). To avoid false infeas at endpoints, use if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12? But if lo=r1+1e-13, hi=r2-1e-13, all points mostly forbidden but endpoints near maybe no exact feasible? For L bisection, approximation ok. Use if lo > r1 and hi < r2: return False. Floating may be okay.
  return True
```

Top-level functions list:

  h = np.array(h0, dtype=float).copy()
  h = project_feasible(h,n)
  c = compute_c_raw(h)
  V = build_V(h,n)

  low=[]; high=[]

  attempt=0

       if time.time() > deadline: break
       r=random

```
def make_gradient(V,c,cmax,alpha=30.0):
    s = alpha*(c-cmax)
    w = np.empty_like(c)
    # threshold: where s > -50 else 0
    np.exp(s, out=w, where=s>-50)? np.exp has where but uninitialized. Use w = np.exp(s); w[s < -40] = 0.0
    tot = w.sum()
    if tot <=0:
       w = (c >= cmax - 1e-9).astype(float)
       tot = w.sum()
    if tot>0: w /= tot
    return V @ w
```
V @ w uses w shape (N,). If V 2D. Good.

`build_V` code:
```
def build_V(h,n):
    N=2*n-1; V=np.zeros((n,N),float); center=n-1
    V[:,center]=1-2*h
    for d in range(1,n):
        L=n-d
        cp=center+d; cn=center-d
        V[:L, cp] += 1.0 - h[d:n]
        V[d:n, cp] -= h[:L]
        V[d:n, cn] += 1.0 - h[:L]
        V[:L, cn] -= h[d:n]
    return V
```
Check for np convention: For lag -d, second derivative +1-h[i+d] for i< L, first derivative -h[i-d] for i>=d. Yes.
Let's verify V row sum not needed.

`update_V`:
```
def update_V(V,r,delta,n):
    idx1 = n-1 + r - np.arange(n)
    idx2 = n-1 + np.arange(n) - r
    V[np.arange(n), idx1] -= delta
    V[np.arange(n), idx2] -= delta
```
Creating arange each call. Could precompute ranges in optimize and pass? For speed, we can avoid allocation by using slices? idx1 is decreasing diagonal idx2 increasing. We can update using loop? `for i, ix in enumerate(idx1)` Python slow. Use `np.arange` allocation length n O(n). Called twice per update, 10k=>14M ints, okay. Could optimize by using `V[rows, idx1]` where rows = np.arange(n) global. Pass rows. In update_V, `rows = np.arange(n)` allocated each call. Better define in optimize_n `rows=np.arange(n)` and pass to update_V. But update_V top-level can accept rows. Let's add param `rows`.
```
def update_V(V, r, delta, rows, n):
    center=n-1
    V[rows, center + r - rows] -= delta
    V[rows, center + rows - r] -= delta
```
`center+r-rows` creates array; fine.
If delta==0 skip.

`project_feasible`: Need avoid long loops. Implement robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
       s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h + diff*room/tot,0,1)
       else:
          tot=s
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h + diff*h/tot,0,1)
    # final tiny adjust maybe
    return h
```
This weighted redistribution may not achieve exact if many clip; 100 enough. Could use simple uniform on free:
```
for _ in range(50):
 diff=target-sum
 free=(h>0)&(h<1)
 if not free.any(): break
 add=diff/ free.sum(); if h[free]+add clipped, etc.
```
Weighted as previous okay.

    return np.interp(x1,x0,h)
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-10
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
    except: h0=np.full(512,0.5); n0=512
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    # maybe quick primary optimize
    primary_deadline = t0 + 0.62*budget_s
    best_h, best_c5 = optimize_n(h0, n0, primary_deadline, rng)
    bN = n0
    # multi try if time
    tries = []
    # generate n candidates: n0, n0-50,n0+50,n0//2, 800,600,400,550,850
    # dedup positive 100..1000
    cand = [bN, bN-50,bN+50,bN-100,bN+100,400,550,650,800,450,750]
    for nt in cand:
       if nt in seen or nt<150 or nt>1000: continue
       if time.time() > deadline - 120: break
       h_start = project_feasible(resample(best_h,bN,nt),nt)
       # maybe also create symmetric/prior for nt? Use best.
       alloctill = min(deadline-80, time.time()+90)
       hT, CT = optimize_n(h_start, nt, alloctill, rng)
       if CT < best_c5: best_h,best_c5,bN = hT,CT,nt
    # final polish best with remaining
    if time.time() < deadline-20:
       hF, CF = optimize_n(best_h, bN, deadline, rng)
       if CF < best_c5: best_h,best_c5,bN = hF,CF
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,bN)
    c5 = compute_c_raw(best_h).max()*2.0/bN
    return best_h, float(c5), bN

Let's write pair_try fully.
Pseudo code in actual:
```
def pair_try(V, c, h, p, q, n, cmax, a_buf, val_buf):
    if p == q: return False, cmax
    tlo = max(-h[p], h[q] - 1.0)
    thi = min(1.0 - h[p], h[q])
    if tlo >= thi - 1e-15: return False, cmax
    center = n-1; N=2*n-1
    d1 = center + q - p; d2 = center + p - q
    np.subtract(V[p], V[q], out=a_buf)
    act = np.where(c >= cmax - 1e-8)[0]
    if act.size == 0: act = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    av = a_buf[act]
    right = float(av.max()); left = -float(av.min())
    can_r = thi > 1e-15; can_l = tlo < -1e-15
    if not ((can_r and right < -1e-11) or (can_l and left < -1e-11)):
        return False, cmax
    aff = np.ones(N, dtype=bool)
    aff[center]=False; aff[d1]=False; aff[d2]=False
    aa = a_buf[aff]; ca = c[aff]
    pos = aa > 1e-13; neg = aa < -1e-13; zero = ~(pos|neg)
    ca_pos = ca[pos].copy(); aa_pos = aa[pos].copy()
    ca_neg = ca[neg].copy(); aa_neg = aa[neg].copy()
    zero_max = float(ca[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e300
    c1=float(c[d1]); a1=float(a_buf[d1]); c2=...; c0=...
    Lhi = cmax
    Llo = cmax - 5.0

Now optimize_n code with rows:
```
def optimize_n(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(np.array(h0,float),n)
    c,V,cmax=rebuild_state(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_cmax=cmax
    dx=2.0/n
    a_buf=np.empty(2*n-1); val_buf=np.empty(2*n-1); rows=np.arange(n)
    tstart=time.time(); last_imp=tstart; last_g=0.0; g_interval=0.8
    low=np.array([],int); high=np.array([],int)
    perturb_count=0; accepted=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       now=time.time()
       if now - last_g > g_interval:
          g=make_gradient(V,c,cmax,30.0)
          low=np.argsort(g)[:8]; high=np.argsort(g)[-8:][::-1]
          last_g=now
       steps=0
       while time.time() < deadline and steps < 40:
          r=rng.random_sample()
          if low.size>0 and r < 0.65:
             p=int(low[rng.randint(low.size)]); q=int(high[rng.randint(high.size)])
          elif r < 0.9:
             p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
          else:
             p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
          if p==q:
             q=(p+1)%n
          imp,newmax=pair_try(V,c,h,p,q,n,rows,cmax,a_buf,val_buf)
          if imp:
             cmax=newmax
             if cmax < best_cmax:
                best_cmax=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
             last_imp=time.time()
             accepted +=1
             if accepted % 150 ==0:
                h = project_feasible(h,n); c,V,cmax = rebuild_state(h,n)
                if cmax < best_cmax: best...
                last_g=0.0
          steps +=1
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       if time.time() - last_imp > 25.0:
          # perturb from best_h
          scale = min(0.03, 1e-4 * (1.6 ** min(perturb_count, 12)))
          h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), n)
          c,V,cmax = rebuild_state(h,n)
          perturb_count +=1
          last_imp = time.time()
          last_g = 0.0
          if perturb_count > 12: perturb_count=0
    h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    cmax = float(compute_c_raw(h).max())
    return h, cmax*2.0/n
```

Now, if first-order skip local but a far nonconvex via two concave? Only one concave. Inactive center far below cannot cause local if active derivatives nonneg. Good.

Let's adjust pair_try skip:
```
center_active = float(c[center]) >= cmax - 1e-8
if center_active and (can_r or can_l):
    pass
else:
    ... skip if no negative derivative
```
But if center_active and active affine with positive derivatives increase, pair solve may still improve via center if gap. Good.

Now, in feasibility, affine mask excludes center. Good.

Potential issue: If p,q pair and d1/d2 convex active with slope zero, they increase quadratically, not help; skip via derivatives maybe if other active derivatives positive? If a convex active slope 0 and center inactive, f locally increases; no improvement. okay.

Let's update pair_try code accordingly.

Another subtle bug: `V @ w` gradient of max raw c; but objective C=dx*c, factor positive. Good.

Now, could use active margins in make_gradient? alpha=30; if center inactive but near, weights. fine.

Let's think if `build_V` for n=1? If n=1, loop none; c len1. Pair p==q skip. But h integral constant 2? n=1 dx=2, sum=0.5? But not used. Ensure n>=2.

Now, final code. Need ensure all imported. Use `math.isfinite` in candidate. Good.

Let's write code in final fenced block. Ensure no long literals. We'll aim <300 lines.

Double-check syntax in pair_try candidate roots addition: We cannot define local helper; just repeat.
Potential repeated code for convex candidate update. We'll write straightforward.

When evaluating candidates, val_buf used later for c update; but after grid fallback, val_buf contains eval; no issue.

If best_t selected but c update with d center -2, d1,d2. What if center coincides with d1 due p-q=0? no. Good.

In optimize_n, after final project best_h, recompute cmax. But if project changes best_h, c5 maybe different. Good.

Multi-n in run: Use `attempt_n` budgets. If primary returns best at n0. Then for each nt, if enough time. Need avoid optimize_n with deadline less than now. Use:
```
if now > deadline - 130: break
h_try = project_feasible(resample(best_h,bN,nt),nt)
end = min(deadline - 90, time.time()+90)
hT,CT=optimize_n(h_try,nt,end,rng)
if CT < best_c5 - 1e-12: best_h,best_c5,bN = hT,CT,nt
```
If `best_h` from nB, resample. Good.
Final polish: `if time.time() < deadline - 20: hF,CF=optimize_n(best_h,bN,deadline,rng); if CF<best_c5: update`.
Then final project.

Potential issue: optimize_n perturbs from best_h; if we call final polish on best_h, it may waste time but okay.

If budget_s is small (e.g. 100), primary_deadline=t0+60, multi maybe none, final. okay.

Now, I will produce final code. Ensure no analysis in final, only code block.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def compute_c_raw(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h.copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(80):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def build_V(h, n):
    N = 2 * n - 1
    center = n - 1
    V = np.zeros((n, N), dtype=float)
    V[:, center] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    for d in range(1, n):
        L = n - d
        cp = center + d
        cn = center - d
        V[:L, cp] += 1.0 - h[d:n]
        V[d:n, cp] -= h[:L]
        V[d:n, cn] += 1.0 - h[:L]
        V[:L, cn] -= h[d:n]
    return V


def update_V(V, r, delta, rows, n):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    center = n - 1
    V[rows, center + r - rows] -= delta
    V[rows, center + rows - r] -= delta


def rebuild_state(h, n):
    c = compute_c_raw(h)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    V = build_V(h, n)
    return c, V, cmax


def resample(h, n0, n1):
    if n0 == n1:
        return h.copy()
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n1)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h)


def make_gradient(V, c, cmax, alpha):
    s = alpha * (c - cmax)
    w = np.exp(s)
    w[s < -36.0] = 0.0
    tot = float(w.sum())
    if tot <= 1e-300:
        w = (c >= cmax - 1e-9).astype(float)
        tot = float(w.sum())
    if tot > 0.0:
        w /= tot
    else:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    return V.dot(w)


def pair_try(V, c, h, p, q, n, rows, cmax, a_buf, val_buf):
    if p == q:
        return False, cmax
    tlo = max(-float(h[p]), float(h[q]) - 1.0)
    thi = min(1.0 - float(h[p]), float(h[q]))
    if tlo >= thi - 1e-15:
        return False, cmax

    center = n - 1
    N = 2 * n - 1
    d1 = center + q - p
    d2 = center + p - q

    np.subtract(V[p], V[q], out=a_buf)

    can_r = thi > 1e-15
    can_l = tlo < -1e-15
    center_active = abs(float(c[center]) - cmax) <= 1e-8

    if not (center_active and (can_r or can_l)):
        act = np.where(c >= cmax - 1e-8)[0]
        if act.size == 0:
            act = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
        av = a_buf[act]
        right = float(av.max())
        left = -float(av.min())
        if not ((can_r and right < -1e-11) or (can_l and left < -1e-11)):
            return False, cmax

    aff = np.ones(N, dtype=bool)
    aff[center] = False
    aff[d1] = False
    aff[d2] = False
    aa = a_buf[aff]
    ca = c[aff]

    pos = aa > 1e-13
    neg = aa < -1e-13
    zero = ~(pos | neg)

    ca_pos = ca[pos].copy()
    aa_pos = aa[pos].copy()
    ca_neg = ca[neg].copy()
    aa_neg = aa[neg].copy()
    zero_max = float(ca[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e300
    pos_cnt = int(ca_pos.size)
    neg_cnt = int(ca_neg.size)

    c1 = float(c[d1]); a1 = float(a_buf[d1])
    c2 = float(c[d2]); a2 = float(a_buf[d2])
    c0 = float(c[center]); a0 = float(a_buf[center])

    Lhi = float(cmax)
    Llo = Lhi - 8.0

    for _ in range(36):
        L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        feasible = False

        if zero_max <= L + 1e-13:
            loI = tlo
            hiI = thi

            if pos_cnt:
                b = float(np.min((L - ca_pos) / aa_pos))
                if b < hiI:
                    hiI = b
            if neg_cnt:
                b = float(np.max((L - ca_neg) / aa_neg))
                if b > loI:
                    loI = b

            if loI <= hiI + 1e-14:
                disc = a1 * a1 - 4.0 * (L - c1)
                if disc < -1e-12:
                    feasible = False
                else:
                    sq = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
                    r1 = (-a1 - sq) * 0.5
                    r2 = (-a1 + sq) * 0.5
                    if r1 > loI:
                        loI = r1
                    if r2 < hiI:
                        hiI = r2
                    if loI > hiI + 1e-14:
                        feasible = False
                    else:
                        disc = a2 * a2 - 4.0 * (L - c2)
                        if disc < -1e-12:
                            feasible = False
                        else:
                            sq = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
                            r1 = (-a2 - sq) * 0.5
                            r2 = (-a2 + sq) * 0.5
                            if r1 > loI:
                                loI = r1
                            if r2 < hiI:
                                hiI = r2
                            feasible = (loI <= hiI + 1e-14)

            if feasible:
                disc0 = a0 * a0 - 8.0 * (L - c0)
                if disc0 >= 0.0:
                    sq = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc0))
                    r1 = (a0 - sq) * 0.25
                    r2 = (a0 + sq) * 0.25
                    if loI > r1 + 1e-13 and hiI < r2 - 1e-13:
                        feasible = False

        if feasible:
            Lhi = L
        else:
            Llo = L

    Lopt = Lhi

    loI = tlo
    hiI = thi
    cands = [tlo, thi, 0.0]

    if pos_cnt:
        b = float(np.min((Lopt - ca_pos) / aa_pos))
        if b < hiI:
            hiI = b
    if neg_cnt:
        b = float(np.max((Lopt - ca_neg) / aa_neg))
        if b > loI:
            loI = b

    disc = a1 * a1 - 4.0 * (Lopt - c1)
    if disc >= 0.0:
        sq = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
        cands.append((-a1 - sq) * 0.5)
        cands.append((-a1 + sq) * 0.5)
    disc = a2 * a2 - 4.0 * (Lopt - c2)
    if disc >= 0.0:
        sq = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
        cands.append((-a2 - sq) * 0.5)
        cands.append((-a2 + sq) * 0.5)

    disc0 = a0 * a0 - 8.0 * (Lopt - c0)
    if disc0 >= 0.0:
        sq = math.sqrt(max(0.0, disc0))
        cands.append((a0 - sq) * 0.25)
        cands.append((a0 + sq) * 0.25)

    cands.append(loI)
    cands.append(hiI)
    cands.append(0.5 * (loI + hiI))

    best_f = float(cmax)
    best_t = 0.0

    for tt in cands:
        if not math.isfinite(tt):
            continue
        if tt < tlo:
            tt = tlo
        elif tt > thi:
            tt = thi
        np.multiply(a_buf, tt, out=val_buf)
        val_buf += c
        tt2 = tt * tt
        val_buf[center] -= 2.0 * tt2
        val_buf[d1] += tt2
        val_buf[d2] += tt2
        f = float(val_buf.max())
        if f < best_f:
            best_f = f
            best_t = tt

    if best_f > Lopt + 1e-7:
        for k in range(1, 33):
            tt = tlo + (thi - tlo) * (float(k) / 32.0)
            np.multiply(a_buf, tt, out=val_buf)
            val_buf += c
            tt2 = tt * tt
            val_buf[center] -= 2.0 * tt2
            val_buf[d1] += tt2
            val_buf[d2] += tt2
            f = float(val_buf.max())
            if f < best_f:
                best_f = f
                best_t = tt

    if best_f >= cmax - 1e-10:
        return False, cmax

    tt = best_t
    tt2 = tt * tt
    np.multiply(a_buf, tt, out=val_buf)
    c += val_buf
    c[center] -= 2.0 * tt2
    c[d1] += tt2
    c[d2] += tt2

    h[p] += tt
    h[q] -= tt

    if h[p] < 0.0:
        h[p] = 0.0
    elif h[p] > 1.0:
        h[p] = 1.0
    if h[q] < 0.0:
        h[q] = 0.0
    elif h[q] > 1.0:
        h[q] = 1.0

    update_V(V, p, tt, rows, n)
    update_V(V, q, -tt, rows, n)
    return True, float(c.max())


def optimize_n(h0, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h0, dtype=float), n)
    c, V, cmax = rebuild_state(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_cmax = cmax
    dx = 2.0 / n

    N = 2 * n - 1
    a_buf = np.empty(N, dtype=float)
    val_buf = np.empty(N, dtype=float)
    rows = np.arange(n)

    t_start = time.time()
    last_activity = t_start
    last_g = 0.0
    low = np.array([], dtype=int)
    high = np.array([], dtype=int)
    accepted = 0
    perturb_count = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        now = time.time()

        if now - last_g > 0.8:
            g = make_gradient(V, c, cmax, 32.0)
            low = np.argsort(g)[:8]
            high = np.argsort(g)[-8:][::-1]
            last_g = now

        steps = 0
        while time.time() < deadline and steps < 45:
            r = float(rng.random_sample())
            if low.size > 0 and r < 0.65:
                p = int(low[int(rng.randint(int(low.size)))])
                q = int(high[int(rng.randint(int(high.size)))])
            else:
                p = int(rng.randint(int(n)))
                q = int(rng.randint(int(n)))

            if p == q:
                p = int(rng.randint(int(n)))
                q = (p + 1) % int(n)

            imp, newmax = pair_try(V, c, h, p, q, n, rows, cmax, a_buf, val_buf)
            if imp:
                cmax = newmax
                last_activity = time.time()
                accepted += 1
                if cmax < best_cmax:
                    best_cmax = cmax
                    best_h = h.copy()
                if accepted % 180 == 0:
                    h = project_feasible(h, n)
                    c, V, cmax = rebuild_state(h, n)
                    if cmax < best_cmax:
                        best_cmax = cmax
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    last_g = 0.0
            steps += 1

        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break

        bad_margin = max(3.0, 0.03 * max(1.0, float(best_cmax)))
        now = time.time()
        if now - last_activity > 8.0 and cmax > best_cmax + bad_margin:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c, V, cmax = rebuild_state(h, n)
            last_activity = now
            last_g = 0.0
            perturb_count = max(0, perturb_count - 1)
        elif now - last_activity > 25.0:
            scale = min(0.035, 1e-4 * (1.6 ** min(perturb_count, 13)))
            h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), n)
            c, V, cmax = rebuild_state(h, n)
            perturb_count = (perturb_count + 1) % 14
            last_activity = now
            last_g = 0.0

    h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    cmax = float(compute_c_raw(h).max())
    return h, cmax * dx


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s)) - 12.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 700
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5, dtype=float)

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)

    phase1_deadline = t0 + 0.62 * max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    if phase1_deadline > deadline:
        phase1_deadline = deadline

    best_h, best_c5 = optimize_n(h0, n0, phase1_deadline, rng)
    best_n = n0

    tries = [
        best_n,
        best_n - 60,
        best_n + 60,
        best_n - 120,
        best_n + 120,
        450,
        550,
        650,
        750,
        850,
        400,
        950,
    ]
    seen = set()

    for nt in tries:
        if time.time() > deadline - 140.0:
            break
        nt = int(nt)
        if nt < 200 or nt > 1000 or nt in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(nt)
        h_try = project_feasible(resample(best_h, best_n, nt), nt)
        end = min(deadline - 90.0, time.time() + 95.0)
        if end <= time.time() + 10.0:
            break
        h_try, c_try = optimize_n(h_try, nt, end, rng)
        if c_try < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c5, best_n = h_try, c_try, nt

    if time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
        h_fin, c_fin = optimize_n(best_h, best_n, deadline, rng)
        if c_fin < best_c5:
            best_h, best_c5, best_n = h_fin, c_fin, best_n

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    c5 = float(compute_c_raw(best_h).max() * (2.0 / best_n))
    return best_h, c5, int(best_n)
```
